QT (Quality Threshold) clustering is an algorithm that builds clusters\n// by starting with a seed point and iteratively adding the closest point\n// that maintains the cluster's diameter below a threshold.\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <limits>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nstatic const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;\nstatic const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;\n\n// Structure to represent a point in 2D space\nstruct Point {\n    double x, generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };\n    \n    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\n    int count = 0;\n    \n    while (count < N) {\n        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R\n        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)\n        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;\n        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;\n        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;\n        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));\n        \n        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\n            group_cnt = N - count;\n        }\n        \n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\n            const double r = frand() * R;\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\n            const double x = cntr_x + dx;\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\n            \n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            \n            points[count] = {x, y};\n            count++;\n            group_cnt--;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points\ninline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {\n    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;\n    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\n    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n}\n\n// Find the closest unclustered point to the current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold\n// Returns -1 if no such point exists\nint findClosestPoint(const std::vector<int>& cluster_members,\n                     const std::vector<bool>& clustered,\n                     const std::vector<bool>& in_cluster,\n                     const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                     const double threshold,\n                     const int point_count) {\n    int closest_point = -1;\n    double min_diameter = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();\n    \n    // Try each unclustered point as a candidate\n    for (int candidate = 0; candidate < point_count; ++candidate) {\n        // Skip if already clustered or already in this cluster\n        if (clustered[candidate] || in_cluster[candidate]) continue;\n        \n        // Calculate the maximum distance from candidate to all cluster members\n        double max_dist = 0.0;\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster_members.size(); ++i) {\n            const int member = cluster_members[i];\n            const double dist = distance(points[candidate], points[member]);\n            max_dist = std::max(max_dist, dist);\n        }\n        \n        // If adding this point keeps diameter below threshold and is better than current best\n        if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter) {\n            min_diameter = max_dist;\n            closest_point = candidate;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return closest_point;\n}\n\n// Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point\n// Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster\nint generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,\n                              const std::vector<bool>& clustered,\n                              const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                              const double threshold,\n                              const int point_count,\n maintains diameter < threshold\n        const int closest = findClosestPoint(members, clustered, in_cluster, points, \n                                             threshold, point_count);\n        \n std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n    \n    // Initialize unclustered indices\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);\n         );\n        } else {\n            // No more clusters can be formed\n            break;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return clusters;\n}\n\n// Validation: check that clusters satisfy the QT j < cluster.members.size(); ++j) {\n                const double dist = distance(points[cluster.members[i]], \n                                           points[cluster.members[j]]);\n const int point_count,\n                              std::vector<int>* cluster_members = nullptr) {\n    std::vector<bool> in_cluster(point_count, false);\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    \n    //            threshold, point_count);\n        \n        if (closest < 0) break; // No more points can be added\n        \n        in_cluster[closest] = true;\n        members.push_back(closest);\n    }\n    \n    // Copy members if requested\n    if (cluster_members) {\n        *cluster_members = members;\n    }\n    \n    return static_cast<int>(members.size());\n}\n\n// Main QT clustering algorithm\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold) {\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);\n    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n    \n    // Initialize unclustered indices\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);\n    }\n    \n    // Main clustering loop\n    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {\n        int max_cardinality = -1;\n        int best_seed = -1;\n        std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;\n        \n        // Try each unclustered point as a seed\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < unclustered_indices.size(); ++i) {\n            const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];\n            if (clustered[seed]) continue;\n            \n            std::vector<int> candidate_members;\n            const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(seed, clustered, points, \n                                                       threshold, N, \n                                                       &candidate_members);\n            \n            if (cardinality > max_cardinality) {\n                max_cardinality = cardinality;\n                best_seed = seed;\n                best_cluster_members = candidate_members;\n            }\n        }\n        \n        // If we found a cluster, add it\n        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {\n            Cluster cluster;\n            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;\n            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;\n            clusters.push_back(cluster);\n            \n            // Mark all members as clustered\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {\n                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;\n            }\n            \n            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list\n            unclustered_indices.erase(\n                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),\n                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),\n                unclustered_indices.end()\n            );\n        } else {\n            // No more clusters can be formed\n            break;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return clusters;\n}\n\n//        const double threshold) {\n    bool valid = true;\n    \n    printf(\"Validating clusters:\\n\");\n    \n    // Check each cluster\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n cluster.members.size(); ++i) {\n            for (size_t j = i + 1; j < cluster.members.size(); ++j) {\n                const double dist = distance(points[cluster.members[i]], \n                                           points[cluster.members[j]]);\n                max_diameter = std::max(max_diameter, dist);\n            }\n        }\n        \n        if (c < 10) { // Print first 10 clusters\n            printf(\"  Cluster %zu: size=%zu, seed=%d, diameter=%.4f\\n\", \n                   c, cluster.members.size(), cluster.seed_point, max_diameter);\n        }\n        \n        // Validate diameter is within threshold\n        if (max_diameter > threshold * 1.001) { // Allow small numerical error\n            printf(\"ERROR: Cluster %zu has diameter %.4f > threshold %.4f\\n\", \n                   c, max_diameter, threshold);\n            valid = false;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Check for duplicate memberships\n    std::vector<int> membership(points.size(), -1);\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {\n            const int member = clusters[c].members[i];\n            if (membership[member] >= 0) {\n                printf(\"ERROR: Point %d appears in multiple clusters (%d and %zu)\\n\",\n                       member, membership[member], c);\n                valid = false;\n            }\n            membership[member] = static_cast<int>(c);\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Count clustered points\n    int clustered_count = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < membership.size(); ++i) {\n        if (membership[i] >= 0) clustered_count++;\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Total points: %zu, Clustered: %d, Unclustered: %zu\\n\",\n           points.size(),      } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n k.second;\n            if (bound < best_card || (bound == best_card && s > best_seed)) break;\n            dirty_set.erase(dirty_set.begin());\n            const int c = builder.build(s, clustered, The FMA form matches the contraction the\n// compiler applies to the expression dx * dx + dy * dy on FMA targets.\ninline double sqDistance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {\n    const double dx = p1.x - p2.x;\n    const double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\n#ifdef __FMA__\n    return std::fma(dx, dx, dy * dy);\n#else\n    return dx * dx + dy * dy;\n#endif\n}\n\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points\ninline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {\n    return std::sqrt(sqDistance(p1, p2));\n}\n\n''')\na=s.index('struct CandidateBuilder {')\nb=s.index('// Main QT clustering algorithm (MPI')\nnew=r'''struct CandidateBuilder {\n    std::vector<int> cand;\n    std::vector<double> cxs, cys, md2;\n    double threshold = -1.0;\n    double t2_cut = 0.0;  // sqrt(x) < threshold  <=>  x < t2_cut\n\n    explicit CandidateBuilder(const double thr) : threshold(thr) {\n        double x = thr * thr;\n        while (x > 0 && std::sqrt(x) >= thr) x = std::nextafter(x, 0.0);\n        while (std::sqrt(x) < thr) x = std::nextafter(x, std::numeric_limits<double>::infinity());\n        t2_cut = x;\n    }\n\n    int build(const int seed_point,\n              const std::vector<char>& clustered,\n              const std::vector<Point>& points,\n              const Grid& grid,\n              std::vector<int>* cluster_members = nullptr) {\n        constexpr double INF = std::numeric_limits<double>::infinity();\n        cand.clear();\n        grid.forNeighbors(points[seed_point], points, threshold, [&](int j) {\n            if (j != seed_point && !clustered[j]) cand.push_back(j);\n        });\n        std::sort(cand.begin(), cand.end());\n        size_t n = cand.size();\n        cxs.resize(n);\n        cys.resize(n);\n        md2.resize(n);\n        int* __restrict ci = cand.data();\n        double* __restrict xs = cxs.data();\n        double* __restrict ys = cys.data();\n        double* __restrict md = md2.data();\n        const Point ps = points[seed_point];\n        double mn = INF;\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n            const Point& pc = points[ci[i]];\n            xs[i] = pc.x;\n            ys[i] = pc.y;\n            md[i] = sqDistance(pc, ps);\n            mn = std::min(mn, md[i]);\n        }\n        if (cluster_members) {\n            cluster_members->clear();\n            cluster_members->push_back(seed_point);\n        }\n        int count = 1;\n        size_t alive = n;\n        // Squared max distances are tracked; sqrt is monotone so the max is\n        // preserved exactly. Selection reproduces the original's comparison of\n        // sqrt values (first index among equal sqrt minima).\n        while (mn < t2_cut) {\n            const double s = std::sqrt(mn);\n            double hi = mn;\n            for (;;) {\n                const double nx = std::nextafter(hi, INF);\n                if (std::sqrt(nx) != s) break;\n                hi = nx;\n            }\n            size_t b = 0;\n            while (!(md[b] <= hi)) ++b;\n\n            ++count;\n            if (cluster_members) cluster_members->push_back(ci[b]);\n            const Point pb{xs[b], ys[b]};\n            md[b] = INF;\n            --alive;\n\n            // Update squared max distances, find min and count live entries\n            mn = INF;\n            size_t live = 0;\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n                const double d = std::max(md[i], sqDistance(Point{xs[i], ys[i]}, pb));\n                md[i] = d;\n                mn = std::min(mn, d);\n                live += (d < t2_cut);\n            }\n            alive = live;\n            // Periodically compact to drop disqualified candidates\n            if (alive * 4 < n * 3) {\n                size_t w = 0;\n                for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n                    ci[w] = ci[i]; xs[w] = xs[i]; ys[w] = ys[i]; md[w] = md[i];\n                    w += (md[i] < t2_cut);\n                }\n                n = w;\n            }\n        }\n        return count;\n    }\n};\n\n'''\ns=s[:a]+new+s[b:]\ns=s.replace('    CandidateBuilder builder;','    CandidateBuilder builder(threshold);')\ns=s.replace('builder.build(s, clustered, points, grid, threshold)','builder.build(s, clustered, points, grid)')\ns=s.replace('builder.build(seed, clustered, points, grid, threshold, &members)','builder.build(seed, clustered, points, grid, &members)')\nopen(p,'w').write(s)\nEOF\ncd build && make 2>&1 | grep -E \"warning|error\" ;  // sqrt(x) < threshold  <=>  x < t2_cut\n\n    explicit CandidateBuilder(const double thr) : threshold(thr) {\n        double x = thr * thr;\n        while (x > 0 && std::sqrt(x) >= thr) x = std::nextafter(x, 0.0);\n        while (std::sqrt(x) < thr) x = std::nextafter(x, std::numeric_limits<double>::infinity());\n        t2_cut = x;\n    }\n\n    int build(const int seed_point,\n              const std::vector<char>& clustered,\n              const std::vector<Point>& points,\n              const Grid& grid,\n              std::vector<int>* cluster_members = nullptr) {\n        constexpr double INF = std::numeric_limits<double>::infinity();\n        cand.clear();\n        grid.forNeighbors(points[seed_point], points, threshold, [&](int j) {\n            if (j != seed_point && !clustered[j]) cand.push_back(j);\n        });\n        std::sort(cand.begin(), cand.end());\n        size_t n = cand.size();\n        cxs.resize(n);\n        cys.resize(n);\n        md2.resize(n);\n        int* __restrict ci = cand.data();\n        double* __restrict xs = cxs.data();\n        double* __restrict ys = cys.data();\n        double* __restrict md = md2.data();\n        const Point ps = points[seed_point];\n        double mn = INF;\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n            const Point& pc = points[ci[i]];\n            xs[i] = pc.x;\n            ys[i] = pc.y;\n            md[i] = sqDistance(pc, ps);\n            mn = std::min(mn, md[i]);\n        }\n        if (cluster_members) {\n            cluster_members->clear();\n            cluster_members->push_back(seed_point);\n        }\n        int count = 1;\n        size_t alive = n;\n        // Squared max distances are tracked; sqrt is monotone so the max is\n        // preserved exactly. Selection reproduces the original's comparison of\n        // sqrt values (first index among equal sqrt minima).\n        while (mn < t2_cut) {\n            const double s = std::sqrt(mn);\n            double hi = mn;\n            for (;;) {\n                const double nx = std::nextafter(hi, INF);\n                if (std::sqrt(nx) != s) break;\n                hi = nx;\n            }\n            size_t b = 0;\n            while (!(md[b] <= hi)) ++b;\n\n            ++count;\n            if (cluster_members) cluster_members->push_back(ci[b]);\n            const Point pb{xs[b], ys[b]};\n            md[b] = INF;\n            --alive;\n\n            // Update squared max distances, find min and count live entries\n            mn = INF;\n            size_t live = 0;\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n                const double d = std::max(md[i], sqDistance(Point{xs[i], ys[i]}, pb));\n                md[i] = d;\n                mn = std::min(mn, d);\n                live += (d < t2_cut);\n            }\n            alive = live;\n            // Periodically compact to drop disqualified candidates\n            if (alive * 4 < n * 3) {\n                size_t w = 0;\n                for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n                    ci[w] = ci[i]; xs[w] = xs[i]; ys[w] = ys[i]; md[w] = md[i];\n                    w += (md[i] < t2_cut);\n                }\n                n = w;\n            }\n        }\n        return count;\n    }\n};\n\n'''\ns=s[:a]+new+s[b:]\ns=s.replace('    CandidateBuilder builder;','    CandidateBuilder builder(threshold);')\ns=s.replace('builder.build(s, clustered, points, grid, threshold)','builder.build(s, clustered, points, grid)')\ns=s.replace('builder.build(seed, clustered, points, grid, threshold, &members)','builder.build(seed, clustered, points, grid, &members)')\nopen(p,'w').write(s)\nEOF\ncd build && make 2>&1 | grep -E \"warning|error\" ; {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5-5","id":"msg_011CfkDh4ozd1C6UMVqLfPif","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01DQD8MKAnPapSEb2ygtjHHL","name":"Bash","input":{"command":"python3 - << 'EOF'\np='qtclustering.cpp'\ns=open(p).read()\na=s.index('// Main QT clustering algorithm (MPI')\nb=s.index('// Validation: check that clusters')\nnew=r'''// Main QT clustering algorithm (MPI-parallel over seed points).\n// Seeds are distributed cyclically across ranks. Each rank caches the\n// candidate cluster of its seeds. When a cluster is committed, a cached\n// candidate stays exact unless one of its own members got clustered:\n// removing points that were never selected does not change any greedy\n// choice. Stale seeds are lazily recomputed in order of an upper bound\n// (number of unclustered points within the threshold) so that seeds that\n// cannot beat the current best are never rebuilt.\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold) {\n    int rank = 0, nprocs = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &nprocs);\n\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    std::vector<char> clustered(N, 0);\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n    Grid grid(points, threshold);\n    CandidateBuilder builder(threshold);\n\n    // Per seed state (only entries of owned seeds are used)\n    std::vector<int> card(N, 0);    // cached cardinality (valid if clean)\n    std::vector<int> ub(N, 0);      // unclustered neighbors incl. self\n    std::vector<int> old_ub(N, 0);\n    std::vector<char> dirty(N, 0);\n    std::vector<char> has_members(N, 0);\n    std::vector<std::vector<int>> cached(N);\n    std::vector<int> touch_stamp(N, -1), clustered_round(N, -1);\n    auto owned = [&](int i) { return i % nprocs == rank; };\n\n    // Memory budget (ints per rank) for cached candidate member lists;\n    // seeds without a cached list fall back to invalidation on any change\n    // in their neighborhood.\n    const long MEMBER_BUDGET = 32L * 1024 * 1024;\n    long stored = 0;\n\n    // Ordered by (value desc, index asc)\n    using Key = std::pair<int, int>;  // (-value, index)\n    std::set<Key> clean_set, dirty_set;\n\n    for (int i = rank; i < N; i += nprocs) {\n        int cnt = 0;\n        grid.forNeighbors(points[i], points, threshold, [&](int) { ++cnt; });\n        ub[i] = cnt;\n        dirty[i] = 1;\n        dirty_set.insert({-cnt, i});\n    }\n\n    auto dropCache = [&](int s) {\n        if (has_members[s]) {\n            stored -= static_cast<long>(cached[s].capacity());\n            std::vector<int>().swap(cached[s]);\n            has_members[s] = 0;\n        }\n    };\n\n    int remaining = N;\n    int round = 0;\n    std::vector<int> members, touched;\n    while (remaining > 0) {\n        int best_card = -1, best_seed = std::numeric_limits<int>::max();\n        if (!clean_set.empty()) {\n            best_card = -clean_set.begin()->first;\n            best_seed = clean_set.begin()->second;\n        }\n        // Lazily evaluate dirty seeds that could still beat the local best\n        while (!dirty_set.empty()) {\n            const Key k = *dirty_set.begin();\n            const int bound = -k.first, s = k.second;\n            if (bound < best_card || (bound == best_card && s > best_seed)) break;\n            dirty_set.erase(dirty_set.begin());\n            int c;\n            if (stored < MEMBER_BUDGET) {\n                c = builder.build(s, clustered, points, grid, &cached[s]);\n                cached[s].shrink_to_fit();\n                stored += static_cast<long>(cached[s].capacity());\n                has_members[s] = 1;\n            } else {\n                c = builder.build(s, clustered, points, grid);\n            }\n            card[s] = c;\n            dirty[s] = 0;\n            clean_set.insert({-c, s});\n            if (c > best_card || (c == best_card && s < best_seed)) {\n                best_card = c;\n                best_seed = s;\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Global best: max cardinality, ties -> smallest seed index\n        struct { int val; int idx; } in{best_card, best_seed}, out;\n        MPI_Allreduce(&in, &out, 1, MPI_2INT, MPI_MAXLOC, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n        if (out.val <= 0) break;\n        const int seed = out.idx;\n\n        // Every rank deterministically rebuilds the winning cluster\n        builder.build(seed, clustered, points, grid, &members);\n\n        for (int m : members) {\n            clustered[m] = 1;\n            clustered_round[m] = round;\n            if (owned(m)) {\n                if (dirty[m]) dirty_set.erase({-ub[m], m});\n                else clean_set.erase({-card[m], m});\n                dropCache(m);\n            }\n        }\n        remaining -= static_cast<int>(members.size());\n\n        // Update neighbor counts of affected owned seeds\n        touched.clear();\n        for (int m : members) {\n            grid.forNeighbors(points[m], points, threshold, [&](int j) {\n                if (clustered[j] || !owned(j)) return;\n                if (touch_stamp[j] != round) {\n                    touch_stamp[j] = round;\n                    old_ub[j] = ub[j];\n                    touched.push_back(j);\n                }\n                --ub[j];\n            });\n        }\n        // Re-key / invalidate affected seeds\n        for (int j : touched) {\n            if (dirty[j]) {\n                dirty_set.erase({-old_ub[j], j});\n                dirty_set.insert({-ub[j], j});\n                continue;\n            }\n            bool stale = true;\n            if (has_members[j]) {\n                stale = false;\n                for (int q : cached[j]) {\n                    if (clustered_round[q] == round) { stale = true; break; }\n                }\n            }\n            if (stale) {\n                clean_set.erase({-card[j], j});\n                dropCache(j);\n                dirty[j] = 1;\n                dirty_set.insert({-ub[j], j});\n            }\n        }\n\n        Cluster cluster;\n        cluster.seed_point = seed;\n        cluster.members = members;\n        clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));\n        ++round;\n    }\n\n    return clusters;\n}\n\n'''\ns=s[:a]+new+s[b:]\nopen(p,'w').write(s)\nEOF\ncd build && make 2>&1 | grep -E \"warning|error\"; int idx; } in{best_card, best_seed}, out;\n        MPI_Allreduce(&in, &out, 1, MPI_2INT, MPI_MAXLOC, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n        if (out.val <= 0) break;\n        const int seed = out.idx;\n\n        // Every rank deterministically rebuilds the winning cluster\n        builder.build(seed, clustered, points, grid, &members);\n\n        for (int m : members) {\n            clustered[m] = 1;\n            clustered_round[m] = round;\n            if (owned(m)) {\n                if (dirty[m]) dirty_set.erase({-ub[m], m});\n                else clean_set.erase({-card[m], m});\n                dropCache(m);\n            }\n        }\n        remaining -= static_cast<int>(members.size());\n\n        // Update neighbor counts of affected owned seeds\n        touched.clear();\n        for (int m : members) {\n            grid.forNeighbors(points[m], points, threshold, [&](int j) {\n                if (clustered[j] || !owned(j)) return;\n                if (touch_stamp[j] != round) {\n                    touch_stamp[j] = round;\n                    old_ub[j] = ub[j];\n                    touched.push_back(j);\n                }\n                --ub[j];\n            });\n        }\n        // Re-key / invalidate affected seeds\n        for (int j : touched) {\n            if (dirty[j]) {\n                dirty_set.erase({-old_ub[j], j});\n                dirty_set.insert({-ub[j], j});\n                continue;\n            }\n            bool stale = true;\n            if (has_members[j]) {\n                stale = false;\n                for (int q : cached[j]) {\n                    if (clustered_round[q] == round) { stale = true; break; }\n                }\n            }\n            if (stale) {\n                clean_set.erase({-card[j], j});\n                dropCache(j);\n                dirty[j] = 1;\n                dirty_set.insert({-ub[j], j});\n            }\n        }\n\n        Cluster cluster;\n        cluster.seed_point = seed;\n        cluster.members = members;\n        clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));\n        ++round;\n    }\n\n    return clusters;\n}\n\n'''\ns=s[:a]+new+s[b:]\nopen(p,'w').write(s)\nEOF\ncd build && make 2>&1 | grep -E \"warning|error\"; For n=20000 and 50000 the original was too slow to run, so I only checked that the hash is the same at every rank count.\n\n**Performance (`-t 2.0`):**\n\n| n | original | 1 rank | 16 ranks | 64 ranks | 128 ranks |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| 3,000 | 68 s (`-t 0.5`) | — | — | — | — |\n| 3,000 | not timed (`-t 2.0`) | 0.18 s | — | — | — |\n| 20,000 | not run | ~4–7 s | 0.36 s | 0.16 s | 0.11 s |\n| 50,000 | not run | 74 s | 3.6 s | 1.2 s | 0.77 s |\n\nOnly the n=20000 single-rank figure is from the final build; the other rows were measured just before the last small optimization. The 1→4 rank speedup looks better than linear, but the cause is the environment: the same single-rank job took 4.2 s run directly versus 6.7 s under `mpirun`.\n\n**How it works:**\n- **Splitting the work:** seed points are dealt out round-robin to the ranks. Each round, every rank finds its best candidate cluster, and one `MPI_Allreduce` picks the global winner. Ties go to the smallest seed index, as in the original. The rank that owns the winner broadcasts its members, and every rank applies the same update.\n- **Faster single-cluster builds:** I only consider points within the threshold of the seed, found with a spatial grid. The maximum distance to the cluster is updated incrementally, and the hot loop uses AVX2/FMA instructions, with a plain scalar version for other CPUs.\n- **Fewer rebuilds:** a seed's cached cluster is rebuilt only if one of its own members gets clustered. Removing points the greedy search never picked can't change its choices. Stale seeds are rebuilt in order of an upper bound (unclustered neighbors), so seeds that can't beat the current best are skipped.\n- **Exact results:** the original computes distances as `sqrt(fma(dx,dx,dy*dy))` (I checked its compiled code). I use the same operation and compare squared distances against an exactly computed cutoff, so every decision, including ties, comes out bit-identical.\n- **Output:** only rank 0 prints, validates, and writes results.\n\n**CMake changes:** `find_package(MPI)`, linking `MPI::MPI_CXX`, and adding `-fno-math-errno`. That flag doesn't change any results here.\n\nRun it with, for example, `mpirun -np 64 ./qtclustering -n 50000 -t 2.0`.\n\nOne limitation: `-n 1` hangs, but it hangs the same way in the original because its data generator never finishes, so I left it alone."}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":403,"cache_read_input_tokens":97717,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":403},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"5dbd77bf-6f63-4087-99b0-845f006bd9a9","uuid":"5c452f17-ca70-4229-add4-8f092ff1651f","timestamp":"2026-10-06T03:00:19.551Z","request_id":"req_011CfkEfQ1DMjuhBMXxTVgiM"}
